- **unix-socket-client**: The Network hand for connect to a local socket with a bounded path. You believe the wire over the log, and you put a deadline on every operation that can hang. A retry without a budget is an outage amplifier; a timeout without a total deadline is a slow hang. Use when the work touches a connection, when the user says "a connection", "a timeout", or "TLS", or when they ask "unix-socket-client", "how do I connect to a local socket with a bounded path", or "unix socket client keeps failing". Also use when a connection must be produced, reviewed, or repaired, or when a previous attempt left it un… ([mrfentmen/meta-os](https://github.com/mrfentmen/meta-os/tree/HEAD/os/skills/unix-socket-client))

- **unix-socket-pipe-diagnosis**: Unix Domain Socket（UDS）与匿名管道（Pipe）故障诊断技能。当用户提到 UDS 连接拒绝、ECONNREFUSED、EADDRINUSE、socket 文件权限错误、管道阻塞、 SIGPIPE 进程退出、socketpair 泄漏、SO_PASSCRED 凭证传递失败、 EACCES、connect 失败、pipe write 卡住 D 状态等关键词时， 必须使用本技能。覆盖场景：UDS listen backlog 满、abstract socket 冲突、 socket 文件权限拒绝、管道缓冲区满写阻塞、SIGPIPE 未处理进程退出、 socketpair FD 泄漏、SCM_RIGHTS 凭证传递异常。 即使只收到"连接不上"、"进程卡住"、"消息发不出"但怀疑 UDS/pipe 相关时 也应触发本技能。 ([openeuler-mirror/witty-diagnosis-agent](https://github.com/openeuler-mirror/witty-diagnosis-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/unix-socket-pipe-diagnosis) · ★7)
- **unix-sockets**: The Linux systems hand for set socket permissions so only the intended user connects. Use when the work touches a unit, when the user says "systemd", "a unit file", or "a boot problem", or when they ask "unix-sockets", "how do I set socket permissions so only the intended user connects", or "unix sockets keeps failing". - **unix-sockets-and-backpressure**: Read before writing or changing the local Unix socket server, per-client outbound queues, or event broadcast/backpressure for the Hyperloop controller.
